**T078 — Hall/transverse transport: the gradient equation predicts the wrong direction**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Mondkar et al. (2603.23498) demonstrate that the QGP under realistic heavy-ion collision conditions has Hall viscosities comparable in magnitude to shear viscosity. Hall viscous response is ẍ ∝ ẑ × ∇φ — perpendicular to the gradient, not along it. The framework's Field primitive ẍ = −∇φ(x) is purely longitudinal: it predicts motion down the gradient. In parity-broken systems, the physical response has a transverse component that can equal or exceed the longitudinal one. This is not insufficiency (the gradient is correct but incomplete) — it is directional error (the predicted direction of response is orthogonal to the actual dominant channel in some configurations). Hall transport is non-dissipative (no entropy production, no energy loss), time-reversal odd, and determined by the interplay of symmetry breaking (O(3) → O(2)) with microscopic scattering. The gradient equation captures none of these features. This is distinct from T006 (stable states not from gradient descent, which concerns endpoints), T029 (anomalous transport universality, which concerns fluctuation statistics), T070 (interaction-network topology, which concerns mode coupling structure), and T043 (propagator blindness, which concerns Green's function structure). T078 concerns the *direction* of macroscopic transport response. The framework's equation points downhill; physics points sideways. The generality of the problem is significant: any system with broken parity — QGP in magnetic fields, rotating superfluids, quantum Hall systems, topological insulators, Weyl semimetals, chiral active matter — exhibits Hall-type transverse response that the gradient primitive is categorically unable to describe. The two independent Hall viscosities (η_H^⊥ and η_H^∥) reflect the reduced O(2) symmetry, with their magnitudes set by the competition between the cyclotron frequency and the scattering rate, a ratio the framework has no primitive to express.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) extend the Field primitive from ẍ = −∇φ to ẍ = −∇φ + Ω × ∇φ (or a more general form including antisymmetric/Hall response), which would require specifying when and how the antisymmetric term appears — fundamentally, this requires a theory of parity breaking that the framework does not have; (b) add a separate "Hall/transverse" primitive governing non-dissipative transport, which would require specifying its relationship to the existing Field and Conservation primitives; or (c) argue that Hall transport is a derived phenomenon reducible to gradient dynamics in an enlarged state space (e.g., Berry curvature in momentum space acts as an effective magnetic field producing Hall response) — but this requires the framework to incorporate Berry phase and momentum-space topology, which are structural features it cannot express (T026). The fact that holographic and kinetic-theory estimates agree on the Hall viscosity magnitude (within parametric factors) adds another instance of T010 (duality), compounding the strain.

**T079 — Spectral flow topology: physical content is encoded in topological invariants of operator families that the framework cannot represent**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Kimura & Sharma (2603.23275) demonstrate that the APS index, spectral flow, and Maslov index on a finite warped cylinder encode physical content (anomalies, fermion number) through topological invariants of operator families parameterized by gauge and geometric data. The framework's Conservation primitive describes what is preserved; the Resolution primitive describes states becoming definite. Neither captures the concept of an integer-valued topological invariant of a *family* of operators. The specific technical finding — that the APS projector is discontinuous at boundary-mode zero crossings, requiring regularization to define continuous spectral flow — reveals that the resolution boundary (the point where a mode transitions from positive to negative spectral region) is generically *singular* in the natural mathematical framework, not smooth. The real-symplectic (Maslov) structure organizing the spectral flow means the topological content of physical operators lives in symplectic geometry, a mathematical category the framework has no contact with. This is distinct from T048 (cohomological completeness of state classification) because T079 concerns not the classification of states but the topological structure of *operator families* — the mathematical objects defining evolution, not the states being evolved. It is distinct from T026 (general structural gap) because it identifies a specific mathematical framework (symplectic geometry / Maslov theory) that organizes a specific class of physical invariants (spectral flow / anomalies) in a way that no amount of dynamical-primitive extension could capture without importing the symplectic framework wholesale.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) incorporate the Maslov index / symplectic geometry framework as a structural element, which would add an entirely new mathematical layer with no connection to the existing five primitives; (b) derive spectral flow and the APS index from Conservation of Computation (since anomalies are related to symmetry and conservation), which would require showing that the topological invariants emerge from the discrete tick structure — a derivation that has never been attempted and for which no path is visible; or (c) accept that the topological content of quantum field theory (anomalies, index theorems, spectral flow) is a category of physical content that the framework is structurally unable to address. The boundary-discontinuity issue is particularly challenging: the point where a spectral mode crosses zero — the "resolution boundary" in the framework's language — is mathematically singular in the APS framework, requiring explicit regularization. The framework has no concept of singular resolution boundaries or their regularization.

---

**T080 — The continuum limit is not unique: the framework has no theory of how discrete structure becomes continuous physics**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Rajasekaran, Yakir & Zhou (2603.24555) prove that the lattice Yang-Mills-Higgs theory with *any* compact connected matrix Lie group G, in the complete symmetry-breaking regime, abelianizes to a Gaussian limit when the lattice spacing → 0 with gauge coupling → ∞ sufficiently fast. The framework's Tick primitive asserts that reality computes in discrete frames, with the tick rate (speed of light) as the maximum propagation speed. The implicit claim is that this discrete computation produces the observed continuous physics in an appropriate limit. Paper 1 proves that the continuum limit depends sensitively on the *relative scaling* of the discretization parameter and the coupling constants, and that one physically relevant scaling (complete symmetry breaking) erases all non-abelian structure — the gauge group G, which distinguishes electromagnetism from the strong force from hypothetical GUT groups, becomes irrelevant. The framework has (a) no theory of which continuum limit is realized, (b) no parameter governing the coupling-to-discretization scaling relationship, (c) no primitive that distinguishes limits preserving algebraic structure from those destroying it, and (d) no account of how the observed non-abelian gauge structure of the Standard Model survives the continuum limit. This is distinct from T070 (interaction-network topology, which concerns the combinatorial structure at a fixed discretization level) because T080 concerns the *fate* of all structure under the limiting process itself. It is distinct from T022 (spectral universality) and T049 (scale-dependent symmetry type) because T080 concerns a specific, proven mathematical theorem about lattice gauge theories, not a general observation about scale dependence. The universality of the result across all compact connected G makes it especially severe: the framework cannot appeal to special properties of any particular gauge group to escape the abelianization.
Why unresolved: The framework has no theory of renormalization group flow, which is the standard physics framework for understanding which continuum limit is realized and what structure it preserves. The Tick primitive says the universe computes in discrete frames but is silent on the mathematical question of how the discrete-to-continuous limit is taken. In physics, this question is answered by the renormalization group, which specifies how parameters (couplings, masses, wavefunction normalizations) must scale with the cutoff to preserve the physical content. The framework would need either (a) a theory of how the tick rate relates to coupling constants under scaling, which would require importing the renormalization group into the framework — effectively adding a "scaling primitive" that determines which features of the discrete computation survive the limit; (b) a declaration that the universe never takes the continuum limit (reality is truly discrete at the Planck scale), which avoids the mathematical issue but then must explain why continuum field theory works so well; or (c) a criterion for selecting the "correct" continuum limit from among the many possibilities, which no primitive provides. Option (b) is the most natural for the framework but then the abelianization theorem becomes a constraint on what can be *approximated* by the continuum theory, and the framework must explain why the observed continuum approximation preserves non-abelian structure — which requires a theory of the scaling relationships it currently lacks.

**T081 — Conservation enforcement has combinatorial prerequisites: the Conservation primitive is structurally incomplete**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Kasai (2603.24588) constructs quantum LDPC codes reaching the Gilbert-Varshamov bound with finite degree, demonstrating that optimal preservation of quantum information requires specific combinatorial code structure (nested CSS pairs, particular degree profiles, relative linear distance conditions). The framework's Conservation of Computation states "computation cannot be created or destroyed, only transformed" as a bare, structureless law. But the *practical enforcement* of this law — maintaining quantum information against noise — requires highly specific combinatorial infrastructure whose structure is invisible to the Conservation primitive. The framework treats conservation as a primitive fact about reality; this paper shows that the *mechanism* of conservation in any realistic (noisy) system is a combinatorial engineering problem with its own optimality conditions (GV bound), structural requirements (LDPC sparsity, nesting), and proof techniques (rigorous computer-assisted verification of distance). The Conservation primitive asserts the *what* (information is preserved) without the *how* (what structural infrastructure is needed), and the *how* turns out to be non-trivial, combinatorial, and essential. This is distinct from T009 (resolution cost structure, which concerns the cost of *producing* definite outcomes) because T081 concerns the cost of *maintaining* conservation against noise. It is distinct from T018 (algebra of computation, which concerns hierarchical organization of operations) because T081 concerns the graph-theoretic/combinatorial prerequisites for information preservation. It is distinct from T046 (combinatorial dimension) because T081 concerns sparsity and degree profiles, not dimensional parameters. The finite-degree condition is specifically sharp: in any physical implementation, each subsystem interacts with only finitely many others, and achieving optimal error correction under this locality constraint is a deep mathematical problem. The framework's Conservation primitive, formulated as a global law, has no contact with the locality constraints that govern its enforcement.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) incorporate the combinatorial structure of error-correcting codes as part of the Conservation primitive's specification, explaining what structural prerequisites are needed for conservation to hold in practice — but this would transform the primitive from a simple universal law into a structured, locality-dependent, combinatorially constrained statement that might not look like a "primitive" at all; (b) declare that conservation holds exactly and that error correction is a practical concern outside the framework's scope — but the framework claims to describe the literal structure of reality, and reality is noisy, so a framework that describes conservation but not its enforcement mechanism is incomplete where it matters most; or (c) add a structural primitive governing the locality and combinatorics of information preservation, connecting to T026, T018, and T070 as further evidence for the structural content gap. The GV bound itself — the information-theoretic limit on how much

---

**T082 — Non-uniqueness of permanently virtual infrastructure: the computation underlying reality is underdetermined even beyond the number field**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Calcagni & Briscese (2603.25709) prove that for fractional field theories with $0 < \gamma < 3$, different self-adjoint formulations of the kinetic term produce different distributions of complex-mass virtual modes but identical physical spectra and identical S-matrix unitarity. The framework's "reality is literally computation" faces a new underdetermination that is *worse* than T069 (number field ℝ vs. ℂ) in several ways: (a) it is a *continuous* family of underdeterminations, not a binary choice; (b) it concerns the computational infrastructure (virtual modes) that T076 showed is constitutively necessary for physical mass generation; (c) it cannot be dismissed as "different descriptions of the same computation" (T010) because the virtual mode spectra are genuinely different mathematical objects — different sets of complex numbers, different residues, different distributions in the complex mass plane — that merely produce the same on-shell physics. The framework must either (a) declare that only the on-shell/resolvable content is "reality" and the virtual infrastructure is ontologically null — but T076 showed this infrastructure generates confinement, making it physically indispensable; (b) declare all formulations equally "real," making reality a multiverse of virtual structures underlying a single set of observations — which is precisely the unfalsifiable metaphysics the framework was designed to avoid; (c) find a physical criterion selecting one virtual mode distribution over others — but the paper proves they produce identical physical spectra, so no physical criterion can exist; or (d) abandon the "reality is literally computation" claim in favor of "reality is literally the equivalence class of computations producing the same resolvable physics" — but this is a substantive weakening, since equivalence classes are abstract mathematical objects, not computations.
Why unresolved: The fakeon prescription, which determines which modes are physical and which are projected out, is the mechanism enforcing the boundary between resolved and permanently virtual. This prescription is a choice made at the level of theory definition, not a consequence of any dynamical process. The framework has no primitive for theory-level choices (T057). The non-uniqueness is exact and proven, not approximate or conjectural, and it applies to gravitational theories ($\gamma > 2$ gives super-renormalizable quantum gravity), meaning it directly concerns the framework's primary example (gravity). The framework is now underdetermined at *three* nested levels: the number field (T069), the propagator power (which γ), and the virtual mode distribution (which self-adjoint formulation), with none of these determinable from within.

**T083 — Vacuum structure as extra-dimensional geometry: the ground state of the strongest attractor is not computational but geometric**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Csáki et al. (2603.25729) demonstrate that the multi-branched θ-vacuum of QCD — including the physical η' mass and the Witten-Veneziano relation — emerges from the geometry of a 5D holographic space: the θ-branches correspond to Wilson line winding, the U(1)_A anomaly is a Stückelberg coupling dual to a Chern-Simons term, and the η' is a zero mode of bulk fluctuations. The framework describes the QCD vacuum as an attractor basin in the strong-force field landscape. But the paper shows this attractor basin's internal structure — its multi-branched topology, its axial anomaly content, its η' mass — is a *geometric* feature of a higher-dimensional space, not a computational output of 4D QCD dynamics. The framework's Attractor primitive says attractor structure determines what a system can produce. Paper 2 shows the attractor structure *is determined by* extra-dimensional geometry and gauge topology (Wilson lines, Chern-Simons terms), inverting the explanatory direction. This extends T054/T059 (ontological inversion) into the strong interaction: even the vacuum of QCD, the system the framework would most naturally describe as "computational" (lattice QCD is literally a computation), has its deepest structural features determined by geometry in a space the framework cannot describe. The Witten-Veneziano relation — $m_{\eta'}^2 \propto \chi_{\text{top}}$ (topological susceptibility) — is derived geometrically from the Stückelberg mass mechanism, meaning the quantitative mass of a physical particle is a topological/geometric invariant of the extra-dimensional theory.
Why unresolved: The framework has no account of extra dimensions, no primitive for holographic correspondence, no mechanism for how 5D geometry generates 4D vacuum structure, and no vocabulary for topological terms (Chern-Simons) that generate physical masses. The holographic derivation is not merely an alternative description (T010) — it provides explanatory content the 4D description lacks. In 4D QCD, the η' mass is a consequence of instantons and the anomaly, which can be calculated but not *explained* in structural terms. In the 5D holographic description, the mass has a transparent geometric origin (Stückelberg coupling from bulk gauge symmetry). The framework's claim that reality is 4D computation is challenged by the observation that the *explanation* lives in 5D geometry. Resolving this would require either (a) incorporating holographic duality as a structural feature of the framework (but holography is a geometric/gravitational phenomenon the framework treats as emergent), (b) demonstrating that the 5D description is merely calculational convenience with no ontological status (but the framework has no criterion for this distinction, as T016 already noted), or (c) expanding the framework to include extra-dimensional structure, which would be a major architectural change with no guidance from the existing five primitives.

---

**T084 — Continuous propagator non-uniqueness: the framework's computational structure is underdetermined along a continuous parameter**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Paper 1 (Calcagni & Briscese 2025) proves that for fractional field theories with kinetic term (□²)^{γ/2}, the propagator structure 1/p^{2γ} is parameterized by a continuous real number γ > 0. For 0 < γ < 3, different self-adjoint formulations of the same fractional power produce different complex-mass virtual mode distributions but identical physical spectra and unitarity. The framework's "reality is literally computation" requires a definite propagator structure, but (a) the physical propagator power γ is a continuous parameter the framework cannot determine (it is not among the listed architectural constants c, ℏ, G, α), (b) even given γ, the virtual mode distribution is non-unique, and (c) the non-uniqueness extends to gravitational theories (γ > 2). This is worse than T069 (binary ℝ/ℂ underdetermination) because it is continuous, and worse than T082 (which noted the non-uniqueness) because the γ parameter itself is undetermined, creating a *two-dimensional* space of underdetermination (γ × self-adjoint formulation choice) where every point produces valid physics. The framework has no primitive, parameter, or mechanism that selects a point in this space.
Why unresolved: The framework has no theory of propagator selection. The fakeon prescription, which resolves the unitarity question for any given formulation, is itself a structural choice (T057) the framework cannot derive. The continuum of γ values connects qualitatively different regimes (non-renormalizable for γ < 1, renormalizable for γ = 1, super-renormalizable for γ > 2) through a continuous parameter, and the framework has no mechanism for determining which regime physical gravity occupies. Adding γ to the list of architectural constants does not help because the self-adjoint formulation choice remains non-unique even at fixed γ.

**T085 — Extra-dimensional geometric determination of 4D attractor structure: the explanatory direction is inverted for the QCD vacuum**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Paper 2 (Csáki et al. 2025) demonstrates that the multi-branched θ-vacuum of QCD, including the U(1)_A anomaly and the η' meson mass (via the Witten-Veneziano relation), emerges as geometric structure of a 5D holographic space. The θ-branches are Wilson line winding numbers; the anomaly is a Stückelberg/Chern-Simons coupling; the η' mass is a bulk gauge mechanism's output. The framework's Attractor primitive describes the QCD vacuum as a deep attractor basin whose structure determines what the system produces. Paper 2 inverts this: the attractor basin's *structure* (branching topology, anomaly content, spectral content) is *determined by* geometry and gauge topology in a higher-dimensional space the framework cannot describe. This is distinct from T059 (general ontological inversion, dynamics from structure) because it concerns a specific, experimentally verified physical system (QCD vacuum, η' mass ≈ 958 MeV) rather than abstract theoretical constructions. It is distinct from T054 (gravity from topological BF theory) because the starting point here is not topological but gauge-geometric in 5D, and the output is not the dynamical theory itself but the vacuum structure of an already-known theory. The framework faces: (a) the strongest attractor in nature has its structure explained geometrically rather than computationally; (b) a measurable particle mass (η') is a topological/geometric invariant of the 5D theory; (c) the 4D computation (lattice QCD) can *calculate* the η' mass but cannot *explain* why the vacuum has multi-branched structure — the explanation requires the 5D description.
Why unresolved: The framework has no account of holographic correspondence, extra dimensions, Chern-Simons terms, Stückelberg couplings, or Wilson line winding. To address this it would need either (a) to demonstrate that the 5D description is derivable from 4D computational principles, reversing the holographic direction (no path visible); (b) to incorporate extra-dimensional structure, which requires new primitives and raises the question of why the framework's "computational substrate" should be 5D rather than 4D; or (c) to dismiss the holographic description as non-ontological calculational convenience — but the framework has no criterion for this distinction (T016), and the holographic description provides explanatory content (structural understanding of vacuum branching, transparent derivation of Witten-Veneziano) that the 4D description lacks. The fact that the η' mass is a measured number (~958 MeV) determined by 5D gauge topology means this tension has direct empirical content, not merely theoretical.

---

**T086 — Local conservation violation: Unimodular Gravity enables matter-vacuum computational exchange**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Alencar & Crispim (2603.30003) construct exact traversable wormhole solutions in Unimodular Gravity where energy-momentum conservation is locally relaxed, with the deficit absorbed by a dynamical cosmological function Λ(x). The framework's Conservation of Computation primitive states "computation cannot be created or destroyed, only transformed." If energy conservation is a special case of this, UG locally violates a special case of the most fundamental primitive. The framework faces: (a) accept local conservation violation, which requires specifying what happens to "computational content" that leaks between matter and vacuum — is the vacuum a computational reservoir? Does it have finite or infinite capacity? Is the exchange reversible? (b) argue that the total (matter + vacuum via Λ(x)) is still conserved, making this a generalized conservation law — but then the primitive must specify that the vacuum participates in conservation, which the framework has not done and which requires the vacuum to carry "computational content," raising the question of what the vacuum computes; (c) argue UG is unphysical — but it produces exact, well-defined solutions with physical matter content and differs from GR only in the trace equation, which is a mild modification. The fact that the local conservation violation *enables* stable non-trivial topology (wormholes) is additionally problematic: the framework would predict that conservation violations produce instabilities or inconsistencies, yet here they produce new stable solutions.
Why unresolved: The framework has no theory of matter-vacuum computational exchange. The Conservation primitive treats "computation" as a single undifferentiated category; UG requires distinguishing matter-sector computation from vacuum-sector computation and allowing transfer between them. This distinction is not available in the framework's current architecture. The connection to T035 (observer-dependent conservation) is significant but distinct: T035 concerns conservation violation for restricted observers in standard GR; T086 concerns conservation violation for the matter sector in a modified theory. The physical viability of UG (it is observationally indistinguishable from GR for most phenomena, differing only in the treatment of the cosmological constant) makes this tension empirically grounded rather than merely theoretical.

**T087 — Topology-induced causal shortcuts: the Tick primitive's local propagation bound is insufficient for global causal structure**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Paper 1 constructs traversable wormholes — spacetimes where two asymptotic regions are connected by a throat, allowing causal signals to travel between regions that would be causally disconnected in the ambient manifold. The Tick primitive sets the speed of light as the maximum propagation rate, and the framework derives causal structure from this local constraint. But wormhole topology creates *global* causal connections that the local tick rate does not determine. Two observers on opposite sides of a wormhole can communicate through the throat at subluminal speeds while being separated by arbitrarily large distances in the ambient geometry — the causal structure is determined by the global topology, not just the local propagation rate. The framework's implicit derivation of causal structure from the Tick is: "tick rate → light cone structure → causal future/past of each event." Wormholes show this is incomplete: "tick rate → local light cone structure" is correct, but "local light cone structure → global causal structure" fails when the topology is non-trivial. The framework needs a global topological input in addition to the local tick rate to determine causal structure, and it has no primitive for global topology. This is distinct from T036 (multi-signature computation, which concerns the signature of spacetime) and T053 (Euclidean nucleation, which concerns the absence of time). T087 concerns a fully Lorentzian spacetime with standard signature and a well-defined tick rate, where the global causal structure differs from what the local tick rate implies due to topological non-triviality.
Why unresolved: The framework would need either (a) a primitive or structural element governing spacetime topology, specifying which global topologies are allowed and how they modify the causal structure determined by the local tick rate; (b) a derivation of topology from the existing primitives (which seems impossible — topology is not a consequence of local dynamics in any known framework, and the framework's primitives are all local); or (c) acceptance that the Tick primitive determines only *local* causal structure and that global causal structure requires additional topological input the framework does not provide. Option (c) is honest but means the framework's causal account is fundamentally incomplete for any spacetime with non-trivial topology (wormholes, cosmological horizons, spatial compactification, topology change).

**T088 — The Grothendieck gap: a universal mathematical constant governs the resolution primitive's quantitative content, and the framework cannot express it**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Jones & Malavolta (2603.30039) prove that the Grothendieck constant K_G strictly exceeds the Davie-Reeds bound, establishing new information about a universal constant that quantifies the maximum advantage

**T089 — The Tick is a quantum, non-ideal reference frame: the framework's most basic primitive is self-undermining at the quantum gravitational level**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Freidel & Kirklin (2604.02228) demonstrate that quantizing the gravitational degrees of freedom on a null ray using the gravitational field itself as a quantum reference frame produces: (a) a non-ideal clock whose coherent states overlap, with overlaps governed by the Kähler potential of Virasoro coadjoint orbits; (b) an observable algebra that is a Virasoro crossed product, not a simple set of definite values; (c) a deformed product on gauge-fixed operators (quantized Dirac bracket) that modifies the fluctuation structure of observables; (d) anomaly cancellation requiring a classical deformation to eliminate spurious degrees of freedom. The framework's Tick primitive says "the universe updates in discrete steps" and "the speed of light is the tick rate." But on the null ray — the tick's own propagation surface — the tick is not discrete, not clean, and not separable from the computation it is supposed to clock. The dressing time QRF is constructed from the gravitational field, making the tick self-referential: the computation provides its own clock, and the clock is part of the computation. The non-ideal character (coherent state overlaps) means no measurement can perfectly distinguish adjacent ticks, introducing irreducible temporal fuzziness governed by infinite-dimensional algebraic structure. The Page-Wootters reduction to the dressing time perspective is the mechanism by which the classical tick emerges, but this reduction is perspective-dependent and non-trivial. This is distinct from T005 (tick-resolution gap, which concerns the timescale separation between tick and resolution), T013 (time dimensionality, which concerns the number of time directions), T053 (Euclidean nucleation, which concerns the tick's absence at the origin), and T036 (multi-signature computation, which concerns intermediate steps in different signatures). T089 concerns the *internal quantum structure* of the tick itself: the tick, when examined at the quantum gravitational level, is an algebraically rich, non-ideal, self-referential quantum object, not a discrete classical parameter.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) replace the Tick primitive with a "quantum tick" that incorporates the Virasoro crossed product structure, non-ideal coherent state overlaps, and QRF-dependence — which would make the primitive so complex that it can no longer serve as a simple foundational axiom; (b) argue that the classical tick emerges via decoherence/Page-Wootters reduction and that the quantum structure is irrelevant for macroscopic physics — which would be true but would mean the framework's most basic primitive is an approximation rather than a literal description, undermining "reality is literally computation" at the most fundamental level; (c) accept that the tick has internal structure governed by Virasoro algebra and Kähler geometry, adding these to the framework's structural content — which would be the most honest option but would require acknowledging that the framework's simplest primitive contains an infinite-dimensional algebraic structure that the framework's architecture cannot express. The deformed product structure (quantized Dirac bracket) is especially challenging because it means that even the composition of observations at the tick level is modified by quantum gravitational effects, and the framework has no account of how observational composition is modified by the primitive's own quantum nature.

**T090 — Correlation structure as a physical organizer independent of and competing with symmetry and conservation**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Pain, Roy, Bardarson & Khaymovich (2604.02321) demonstrate that long-range correlations in hopping amplitudes create algebraic localization that is robust against finite time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB). The localization persists until TRSB exceeds a critical threshold, at which point all states delocalize. The framework's primitives include Conservation (which generates symmetry-based constraints) and Field (which describes gradient dynamics), but neither captures *correlation structure* — the statistical relationships between coupling parameters across the system. In this system, correlation structure competes with symmetry: correlations protect localization while TRSB destroys it, and the outcome is determined by the quantitative balance between the two. The framework has no primitive for correlation structure

---

**T091 — The Tick is derived, not primitive: the framework's foundational axiom is an emergent approximation**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Freidel & Kirklin (2604.02228) prove that the gravitational degrees of freedom on a null ray, when quantized using the gravitational field itself as a quantum reference frame, produce an observable algebra (Virasoro crossed product), a non-ideal clock (overlapping coherent states), and a frame-dependent vacuum prescription (covariant normal ordering). The classical discrete tick emerges only through Page-Wootters reduction to the dressing time perspective — a non-trivial, perspective-dependent operation. The framework declares the Tick primitive ("the universe updates in discrete steps") as its most fundamental axiom. Paper 1 demonstrates this is an emergent description, not a fundamental one. The tick's internal quantum structure is: (a) continuous (non-ideal QRF, no sharp tick boundaries), (b) infinite-dimensional (Virasoro algebra), (c) self-referential (the clock is made of the field being clocked), (d) perspective-dependent (covariant normal ordering depends on QRF choice). A primitive that is emergent, infinitely structured, self-referential, and frame-dependent is not a primitive in any meaningful sense. This subsumes and deepens T089 (which flagged the issue) by providing the complete technical demonstration. It is distinct from T005 (tick-resolution gap, about timescale separation), T013 (time dimensionality, about the number of time directions), T053 (Euclidean nucleation, about the tick's absence at the origin), and T036 (multi-signature computation, about intermediate signatures). T091 concerns the *ontological status* of the tick itself: it is derived from deeper quantum gravitational structure, not a starting point for derivation.
Why unresolved: The framework's entire architecture depends on the Tick as its first primitive — the discrete update step from which all temporal structure follows. If the tick is emergent, the framework needs a pre-tick foundation: what is the structure from which the tick emerges? The paper's answer — the Virasoro crossed product algebra of gravitational degrees of freedom on a null ray, with the Page-Wootters mechanism generating the classical time perspective — is entirely outside the framework's vocabulary. Adopting this as the foundation would require replacing the five dynamical primitives with algebraic/structural primitives (Virasoro algebra, Kähler geometry, QRF theory), which would be a complete rewrite. The alternative — maintaining the tick as a "good enough" macroscopic approximation — is viable for practical purposes but abandons the framework's distinctive claim that its primitives are literal descriptions of reality's fundamental structure.

**T092 — Singularities as structured boundaries: the framework has no concept of computational termination with well-defined boundary data**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Franco-Grisales (2604.02104) proves that quiescent big bang singularities form locally from generic initial data and induce well-defined geometric initial data on the singularity surface itself. The framework's existing concepts for singularities are: (a) substrate breakdown (all computation fails — T040), (b) field-specific breakdown (one field diverges, others survive — T044), (c) causally screened singularity (singularity invisible to boundary computation — T064). Paper 2 introduces a fourth category: (d) structured termination — the computation terminates (curvature blows up) but the termination boundary carries well-defined geometric data that completely specifies the singularity's structure. The framework has no concept of termination-with-boundary-data. Its Resolution primitive treats states as either resolved or unresolved; its Conservation primitive treats computation as persistent. A singularity that simultaneously ends the computation and provides a complete geometric description of the ending is a new ontological category. The localization of the result (the singularity forms from local data on an open set) means this is not a global catastrophe requiring global description but a local phenomenon determined by local initial data — the singularity is as "local" as the framework's gradient dynamics, yet it produces a geometric boundary the framework cannot represent. The matter-model independence of the formulation (the time function and symmetric hyperbolic system work for any matter content) suggests this structured-termination phenomenon is universal to Einstein gravity, not special to particular matter couplings.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) extend its ontology to include "computational boundaries with induced data," which would require specifying what it means for a computation to terminate while leaving structured information at the termination surface — is this information part of the conserved computational content? Does it represent a final resolution? Is it substrate-level architecture? (b) Reinterpret the singularity as not a termination but a boundary condition connecting to another computational domain (a "bounce" or "transition"), which would require a theory of computational domain boundaries the framework does not have; or (c) treat the geometric data on the singularity as the "seed" of the computation's origin — connecting to T002 (origin problem) by suggesting that the big bang singularity's geometric data is the initial computational specification. Option (c) is the most interesting but would require the framework to develop a theory of how geometric boundary data on singularities relates to the initial conditions of the computation, which currently has no path through the existing primitives.

**T093 — The singularity time function: physical time near computational termination is defined by a structural equation, not by the tick**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Franco-Grisales introduces a new time function near the big bang singularity that satisfies a specific second-order ODE. This time function simultaneously (a) synchronizes the singularity (makes it occur at a single time), (b) yields a symmetric hyperbolic formulation of Einstein's equations (ensuring well-posedness), and (c) is independent of the matter model. The framework's Tick primitive assumes a universal time structure — discrete updates at the speed of light. Near the big bang singularity, the physically meaningful time structure is not the Tick but this geometrically defined time function, which has no discrete character and is determined by a differential equation on spacetime. The framework has no mechanism for the tick to adapt its structure near singularities or for the tick to be replaced by a geometrically determined time function in extreme regimes. This is distinct from T091 (tick is derived at the quantum level) because T093 concerns the *classical* regime

---

**T094 — Indefinite causal order: the Tick primitive presupposes a structure that quantum mechanics does not guarantee**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Apadula, Grinbaum & Brukner (2604.02873) demonstrate that quantum processes can have indefinite causal order, where the temporal sequence of events is itself in superposition. The framework's Tick primitive assumes a definite sequential ordering of computational updates. The process matrix formalism describes physically realizable processes (the quantum switch has been experimentally demonstrated) where no such definite ordering exists. The perspective-neutral description is a higher-order object admitting multiple incompatible time foliations, related by unitary maps that reshuffle past and future. The framework faces: (a) the Tick's sequential structure is not merely an approximation that breaks down at high energies (T089/T091) but is conceptually inadequate for describing indefinite-causal-order processes at any energy scale; (b) the Resolution primitive's "arrow of time as accumulation of resolved states" is foliation-dependent — different frame perspectives produce different resolution histories of the same process; (c) the paper's positive construction (extending the process with QRF subsystems to provide a shared spatiotemporal scaffold) shows that definite causal order is a *resource* that must be supplied by additional quantum systems, not a primitive feature of reality. The framework treats causal order as given by the Tick; the paper treats causal order as a quantum degree of freedom that can be superposed, measured, and transformed. These are incompatible ontological commitments. This is distinct from T089/T091 (which concern the tick's internal quantum structure at the Planck scale) because T094 concerns the *existence* of a definite causal order at *any* scale for certain quantum processes. It is distinct from T062 (orientation reversal in dS quotient spacetimes) because T094 concerns quantum indefiniteness of causal order, not classical reversal of temporal orientation.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) replace the Tick primitive with a "causal structure primitive" that can be definite or indefinite, ordered or superposed — but this would make the primitive so general that it loses its explanatory force (if causal order can be anything, the Tick constrains nothing); (b) restrict the framework's scope to definite-causal-order processes, acknowledging that indefinite-causal-order physics (quantum switch, process matrices) is outside its domain — but the quantum switch has been experimentally realized, so this restriction excludes real physics; (c) argue that the perspective-neutral process matrix is a mathematical abstraction and that "real" physics always has a definite causal order from some perspective — but the paper's no-go results show that no single perspective can accommodate all the physical predictions, and the choice of perspective is conventional, not physical. The paper's extended-QRF construction is particularly challenging: it shows that definite causal order *can* be recovered, but only by adding quantum reference frame subsystems that provide the temporal scaffold — meaning the Tick is not primitive but must be constructed from quantum systems, echoing T091 at the operational (not just Planck-scale) level.

**T095 — Black hole mass as topological charge: the deepest attractor's defining quantity is topological, not dynamical**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Sengupta (2604.02895) constructs singularity-free, matterless "bubble spacetimes" where the exterior geometry is identical to a Schwarzschild or Schwarzschild-dS/AdS black hole, but the interior is replaced by a degenerate metric phase. The mass parameter is a topological charge — a winding number of the phase boundary between degenerate and nondegenerate metric sectors. The topologically non-trivial boundary is at the photon sphere (r = 3M for Schwarzschild), not the event horizon (r = 2M). The event horizon is topologically trivial (zero topological charge). The framework describes black holes as "attractor basins so deep that escape velocity exceeds the tick rate" and attributes the mass to the depth of the energy well. Paper 2 demonstrates that mass can instead be a topological invariant of a metric phase boundary — a discrete, integer-valued, structural quantity, not a continuous dynamical one. The framework faces: (a) the primary physical quantity defining its deepest attractor (mass) is topological, not dynamical — determined by the topology of a phase boundary, not by energy accumulated at a minimum; (b) the topologically significant surface is the photon sphere (the attractor's saddle point), not the event horizon (the attractor's boundary), inverting the framework's assignment of ontological significance; (c) the degenerate metric interior is a region where the framework's primitives are undefined (no well-defined distances, times, or causal structure), yet this region is essential to the construction — the topological charge exists because the degenerate phase exists; (d) the universality of the photon sphere's topological significance (it persists with arbitrary Λ) suggests this is a fundamental feature, not a special case. This is distinct from T047 (scrambling extremality at merger remnants, which concerns dynamics) and T092 (structured termination at singularities, which concerns boundary data). T095 concerns the *definition* of the attractor's most basic quantity (mass) and the *location* of its topological content (photon sphere, not horizon).
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) incorporate a theory of metric degeneracy phases and their topological interface charges, which requires going far beyond the current primitives into first-order gravity formalism (tetrad/vierbein theory) where the metric degeneracy is a natural geometric feature — but the framework has no contact with this formalism; (b) reinterpret "

**T096 — The Ehrenfest boundary: quantum reversibility vs. classical irreversibility is controlled by a timescale the framework cannot express**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Paper 2 demonstrates that the same Hamiltonian dynamics (kicked harmonic oscillator) produces perfect time-reversibility in the quantum regime and exponential sensitivity / irreversibility in the classical regime, with the crossover controlled by the Ehrenfest time $t_E \propto \ln(1/\hbar)/\lambda$ where $\lambda$ is the Lyapunov exponent. The framework's Resolution primitive attributes the arrow of time to quantum measurement/resolution. Paper 2 shows that prior to resolution, quantum evolution is perfectly reversible — confirming that the pre-resolution state has no arrow. But the framework has no account of the quantum-to-classical crossover that *generates* the classical arrow. The Ehrenfest time depends on the ratio of two quantities: the tick-level structure (ℏ, determining the minimum phase-space cell) and the dynamical instability (λ, the Lyapunov exponent measuring chaotic amplification). Neither ℏ alone (Tick primitive) nor the dynamical landscape alone (Attractor/Field primitive) determines the crossover — it is the *logarithmic ratio* of the two that sets the timescale. The framework has no primitive, parameter, or mechanism for this ratio. This is distinct from T005 (tick-resolution gap, which concerns the timescale for hydrodynamic equilibration) because T096 concerns the timescale for the *emergence of irreversibility itself* — the point where the arrow of time turns on. It is distinct from T075 (PIR as an independent source of irreversibility) because T075 identified the mechanism while T096 identifies the specific crossover timescale and the fact that it depends on a combination of quantities from different primitives (ℏ from the Tick, λ from the Attractor/Field dynamics) that the framework cannot compose.

Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) develop a theory of the quantum-classical crossover that expresses how the tick-level structure (ℏ) interacts with the dynamical landscape (Lyapunov exponents) to produce irreversibility, which requires a composition of the Tick and Field primitives that the framework's architecture does not support — they are independent primitives with no interaction term; (b) incorporate the Ehrenfest time as a derived quantity from the primitives, which requires specifying how to extract Lyapunov exponents from the gradient equation (possible in principle but never done within the framework); or (c) accept that the onset of irreversibility is determined by a quantity ($t_E$) that the framework can neither derive nor express, meaning the framework's account of the arrow of time is not only non-exhaustive (T075) but is missing the timescale governing its own validity domain.

**T097 — Regularity class of resolution: the framework's Resolution primitive is under-specified where it matters most**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Paper 1 (Luk & Sbierski 2604.04877) definitively establishes that Strong Cosmic Censorship for generic Kerr black holes holds at the Lipschitz level but fails at the $C^0$ level. The physical question — does deterministic computation survive past the Cauchy horizon? — has the answer "it depends on what regularity class you demand for 'deterministic.'" The framework's Resolution primitive treats resolution as binary: unresolved → definite. It has no concept of "how definite." Yet the most fundamental question about the interior of the framework's deepest attractor (black holes) — whether the computation can continue past an internal boundary — is entirely determined by this missing concept. If "definite" means $C^0$ (continuous metric), the computation passes through into a causally pathological region with non-unique evolution. If "definite" means Lipschitz (bounded first derivatives), the computation terminates at the Cauchy horizon with Lipschitz-inextendibility. If "definite" means $C^2$ (twice differentiable, required for the Einstein equations to hold classically), the computation terminates even sooner (the curvature blows up before the Cauchy horizon). Each regularity class gives a *different physical answer* to the question "what happens inside a black hole?" The framework cannot even formulate this question because it lacks regularity classes. This tension is distinct from T067 (which flagged the regularity gap as an issue) because Paper 1 now provides the *definitive proof* for Kerr (not just Reissner-Nordström, which was the basis for T067). It is distinct from T092 (structured termination at singularities, which concerns big bang singularities with induced data) because T097 concerns an *internal* boundary within a continuing spacetime, not a terminal boundary. The $C^0$-but-not-Lipschitz result is especially problematic: it means there exists a mathematical extension of the spacetime past the Cauchy horizon (the metric is continuous), but no physically meaningful extension (the Einstein equations cannot be formulated, even weakly, past the Lipschitz threshold).

Why unresolved: The framework would need to specify a regularity class for "definite" — i.e., what level of mathematical smoothness is required for a state to count as "resolved." This is not merely a technical refinement; it is a foundational specification that determines the

---

**T098 — Inter-observable response structure: the gradient equation is blind to universal relational constraints between observables**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Zheng & Lu (2604.06162) prove that in Markov jump processes, altering a single edge's transition rate produces mutual linearity — any two observables (state populations, currents, counting observables) are linearly dependent on each other in the long-time limit, and this extends to non-stationary relaxation dynamics for state and counting observables. The framework's Field primitive ẍ = −∇φ(x) describes how individual variables respond to potentials but says nothing about the *relational structure between different observables* under perturbation. Mutual linearity is a universal constraint on this relational structure: it holds regardless of distance from equilibrium, regardless of system complexity, regardless of whether the system is stationary or relaxing. The constraint emerges from the trajectory-level factorization of path weights — a combinatorial structural property of how trajectories through the network decompose when a single edge is perturbed. The framework has no primitive, parameter, or mechanism for inter-observable response relations. This is distinct from T070 (interaction-network topology, which concerns which modes couple to which) because T098 concerns the *response structure* of the network under perturbation, not its coupling topology. It is distinct from T029 (anomalous transport universality, which concerns fluctuation statistics) because T098 concerns deterministic response relations, not stochastic fluctuations. It is distinct from T078 (Hall transport directionality) because T098 concerns universal *constraints* on response, not the direction of response. The generalizability to diffusion processes and open quantum systems means this is not a peculiarity of discrete Markov chains but a universal feature of stochastic dynamics that the framework's dynamical primitives cannot access.

Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) derive mutual linearity from the gradient equation or some combination of the five primitives, which appears impossible since the gradient equation describes individual-observable dynamics while mutual linearity constrains inter-observable relations — a categorically different level of description; (b) add a structural primitive governing the relational/response structure of observable networks, which would require specifying what determines the topology and strength of inter-observable constraints — essentially a theory of how the computation's response structure is organized; or (c) incorporate trajectory-level combinatorial analysis as a framework tool, which would require going beyond the gradient equation to a path-integral or trajectory-ensemble description of dynamics. Option (c) is the most natural but would represent a significant extension: the framework currently describes motion (gradient descent) and endpoints (attractors) but not the trajectory-ensemble structure that determines response relations.

---

**T099 — EFT operator basis as structural determinant of observables: the gradient equation cannot distinguish interaction architectures**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Ghosh, Maity & Ullah (2604.06145) demonstrate that the sign of the equilateral inflationary bispectrum from massive scalar exchange is not universal but depends on the relative coefficients of independent cubic interaction operators in the EFT of inflation. The leading boost-breaking operator alone gives a universally negative equilateral bispectrum; additional operators generate competing contributions that can flip the sign. The critical ratio of interaction coefficients separating positive from negative bispectrum is a structural parameter of the EFT. The framework's Field primitive ẍ = −∇φ(x) describes the inflaton's motion down its potential gradient but is completely silent on which cubic interaction structures exist, what their relative coefficients are, and how they compete to determine the bispectrum's sign. The bootstrap construction (de Sitter-invariant seed function → weight-shifting operators → soft-limit procedure) demonstrates that the natural language for this physics is algebraic/group-theoretic (conformal symmetry, weight-shifting), not dynamical (gradient descent). The reduced sound speed $c_s < 1$ modifying the critical ratio is a parameter the framework would need to interpret as a local modification of the effective tick rate for inflaton fluctuations, but this interpretation provides no predictive content — it cannot determine $c_s$ or its effect on the critical ratio. The multi-particle exchange result (positive bispectrum possible even when higher-order operators are subdominant) further demonstrates that the *spectrum of exchanged particles* interacts with the operator basis in ways the gradient equation cannot capture.

Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) extend the Field primitive to include the full EFT operator basis — not just the leading gradient term but all independent cubic, quartic, etc. interaction structures with their relative coefficients — which would transform the primitive from a single equation into a structured catalogue of allowed interactions; (b) derive the EFT operator basis from some combination of the five primitives and the substrate architecture, which would require a theory of why the inflationary EFT has the operators it has — a question the framework has no resources to address; or (c) accept that the observable consequences of field dynamics (bispectrum sign, shape, amplitude) depend on structural information (EFT operator basis, coupling ratios) that the gradient equation does not contain, making the Field primitive a necessary but radically insufficient description of field physics. Option (c) is the most honest and aligns with the accumulating evidence (T070, T043, T078, T098) that the gradient equation captures only the leading-order mean-field behavior while the physically distinctive features are determined by structural content the framework cannot express.

---

**T100 — Phase space is algebraically derived: the framework's dynamical arena is not primitive**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Ali & Stettinger (2604.07334) demonstrate that the symplectic form defining phase space — the mathematical structure presupposed by all five of the framework's primitives — is derivable from L∞-algebraic structure of the Lagrangian via the covariant phase space method. The BEF symplectic form handles non-local theories (arbitrary derivative order), coincides with the standard Barnich-Brandt construction for second-order theories, and naturally encodes corner terms and boundary conditions. The framework's five primitives (Tick, Field, Attractor, Resolution, Conservation) are all statements about dynamics on phase space: gradient descent occurs in phase space, attractors are phase space features, conservation laws are phase space invariants, the Tick generates phase space evolution. If the phase space itself is an algebraic construct, then all five primitives are statements about phenomena occurring within an algebraically derived arena, not statements about fundamental reality. This is deeper than T059 (ontological inversion of specific theories) because it concerns the *arena* rather than the *actors*: T059 showed that gravity and conservation laws are derived from structure; T100 shows that the *space in which gravity and conservation laws are defined* is derived from structure. The L∞ origin of the Hamiltonian (the generator of time evolution) means the Tick primitive's dynamical content is algebraically determined, reinforcing T091 (tick as derived) from a completely independent direction. The non-local extension (BEF handles infinite-derivative theories) connects to T084 (continuous propagator non-uniqueness): the phase space for fractional/non-local field theories is algebraically well-defined via the BEF construction, even though the framework's gradient equation cannot distinguish these theories.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) demonstrate that L∞-algebraic structure emerges from the five primitives, which would require the primitives to generate the very phase space they are defined on — a bootstrapping problem with no visible solution; (b) add L∞ structure as a foundational element prior to the five primitives, which would mean the framework's starting point is algebraic, not computational, and the primitives are derived consequences of algebraic structure; or (c) argue that phase space is part of the substrate architecture (not derivable from within), but Paper 1 derives it from the Lagrangian (which is within the computation), directly contradicting this escape. The corner term result is additionally problematic: the boundary/corner structure that T045 identified as necessary scaffolding for conservation laws is *automatically encoded* in the L∞ symplectic structure, meaning the scaffolding is algebraic, not geometric — which resolves T045 in favor of algebraic primacy rather than geometric primacy, but at the cost of further subordinating the framework's primitives.

**T101 — Fractonic mobility constraints: the gradient exists but motion is directionally forbidden**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Furukawa (2604.07293) studies a 2+1d fractonic field theory where excitations have restricted mobility — they cannot move freely in all spatial directions despite the existence of nonzero gradients in forbidden directions. The framework's Field primitive ẍ = −∇φ(x) assumes that the system responds to the gradient by moving along it. Fractonic systems violate this: the gradient is well-defined, its direction is well-defined, but the system's response is *restricted to specific submanifolds* by subsystem symmetries (momentum and winding subsystem symmetries in the φ-theory). This is categorically distinct from all previously identified gradient primitive failures: T070 (gradient correct but insufficient — interaction network determines physics), T078 (gradient predicts wrong direction — Hall transport is perpendicular), T006 (gradient descent misses stable excited states), T043 (gradient blind to propagator structure). T101 identifies a new failure mode: the gradient equation is *correct in its local form* but *inapplicable in certain directions* due to higher-rank gauge constraints. The subsystem symmetries that enforce mobility restrictions are not captured by any primitive: they are neither standard symmetries (T032) nor conservation laws in the usual sense — they are directionally restricted conservation laws that hold on sub-manifolds of spacetime. The framework has no concept of directional or subsystem conservation.
Why unresolved: The framework would need to either (a) extend the Field primitive to include mobility constraints, specifying which directions in the configuration space are dynamically accessible — this would require incorporating the subsystem symmetry structure, which is a higher-rank gauge-theoretic concept far beyond the gradient equation; (b) derive mobility constraints from the existing primitives, which is impossible since the gradient equation treats all directions equally; or (c) add a new primitive governing subsystem/directional conservation structure, which connects to but is distinct from T032 (symmetry as missing primitive) because subsystem symmetries have a spatial/foliation structure that standard symmetries lack.

**T102 — Topological charge transmutation: topology redefines the identity of conserved quantities**
Opened: this cycle
Description: Furukawa (2604.07293) demonstrates generalized Witten effects where vortex operators carrying winding subsystem charge acquire momentum subsystem charge due to topological theta terms. In the foliated case, the acquired charge has quadrupolar structure. The framework's Conservation of Computation treats conserved quantities as fixed categories: computational content is conserved but its *type* is stable. The Witten effect shows that topological structure can *transmute* the charge type of an excitation — a vortex that carried only winding charge now also carries momentum charge, with the transmutation mediated by a topological term that does not affect the classical equations of motion. The framework has no mechanism for: (a) topological terms that modify quantum theory without modifying classical gradient dynamics; (b) charge transmutation where the conserved quantity's *identity* changes due to topology; (c) higher-multipole (quadrupolar) charge structures; (d) spatially varying topological parameters (the foliated theta angle varies spatially) that produce no classical effects but modify the charge

**T103 — Boundary algebraic organization: the framework's own terminal surface has constitutive structure the framework cannot express**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Nenmeli & Zhu (2604.08498) demonstrate that at null infinity — the surface where the Tick primitive's propagation terminates — the complete perturbative content of gauge theory and gravity is organized by a differential operator algebra: loop amplitudes are differential operators acting on tree-level Carrollian amplitudes, extending to all loop orders via BDS in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. Gravitational eikonal amplitudes develop Carroll-time logarithms whose discontinuities are descendants of Born-level amplitudes. IR divergences factorize naturally in the Carrollian representation. The framework's Tick sends computation to null infinity but has no account of the algebraic organization that the computation exhibits when it arrives. This is distinct from T045 (which noted conservation requires geometric scaffolding at infinity) because T103 concerns not scaffolding for conservation but the *complete algebraic organization of all scattering content* at the boundary — including the loop/tree relationship, the descendant structure for gravitational tails, and the factorization enabling IR safety. It is distinct from T026 (general structural content gap) because T103 identifies structural content at the *Tick's own boundary* — the one surface the framework's most basic primitive directly constructs. The framework generates null infinity through propagation at the tick rate but cannot describe what the computation looks like when it gets there. The differential operator structure (loop = operator × tree) means the boundary computation is not "more computation accumulated from more ticks" but "algebraic operations applied to the simplest computational seed," inviting a description where the physical content is generated by algebraic action, not temporal accumulation.

Why unresolved: The framework would need either (a) a theory of how the tick's propagation generates Carrollian conformal symmetry and its differential operator algebra at the boundary, which would require deriving the BMS/Carrollian structure from the discrete tick — no path is visible; (b) a declaration that null infinity is outside the framework's scope — but null infinity is where all scattering measurements occur and where the tick's propagation naturally terminates, making this an unacceptable exclusion; or (c) acceptance that the boundary of the computation has constitutive algebraic structure independent of and prior to the computation itself, extending T059 (ontological inversion) to the framework's own boundary. Option (c) is the most honest and creates a specific version of the ontological inversion: the Tick generates the boundary, but the boundary's structure is not generated by the Tick.

---

**T104 — Supersymmetric completion of geometric objects: physical completeness requires algebraic operations the framework cannot represent**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Huang, Kuo, Liu & Mei (2604.08512) demonstrate that the orthogonal Grassmannian OG(k,2k) provides homogeneous solutions to conformal Ward identities for cosmological WFCs, but for conserved currents (spinning particles), the Grassmannian produces only *discontinuities* — incomplete answers. The full WFC requires augmentation by a supersymmetric kinematic prefactor derived from $\mathcal{N}=2$ SUSY relations between spinning and non-spinning sectors. The framework's computational picture — reality computes tick by tick, moving down gradients, resolving states — has no mechanism for "completing" an answer via supersymmetric relations. The completion is not a dynamical process (no additional computation is performed); it is a structural operation (SUSY relates two sectors and the relation provides the missing piece). The positive and negative branches of the Grassmannian formula correspond to distinct supersymmetric invariants and produce distinct helicity amplitudes in the flat-space limit, meaning the branching structure is physically meaningful — different branches produce different observable physics — and is determined by the geometry/algebra of the Grassmannian and SUSY, not by any dynamical primitive. This is distinct from T016 (geometric encoding vs. computational ontology, which concerns the Grassmannian as an alternative description) because T104 concerns the *insufficiency* of the Grassmannian alone — the geometric object needs algebraic supplementation to produce complete physical answers. It is distinct from T050 (cluster algebra as ontological competitor) because T104 concerns the interplay *between* geometric and algebraic structures, not a single alternative framework. The framework faces a compound problem: neither the geometric ingredient (Grassmannian) nor the algebraic ingredient (SUSY completion) nor their combination is expressible in terms of the five primitives, and both are necessary for physically complete answers.

Why unresolved: The framework would need either (a) a mechanism for how "geometric computation" (Grassmannian) is "algebraically completed" (SUSY prefactor) within the tick-based computational picture — but SUSY completion is not a temporal process and cannot be placed within the tick sequence; (b) a primitive governing the relationship between geometric and algebraic organizational structures, which would be a meta-structural primitive beyond even the structural primitives T026 identifies as missing; or (c) acceptance that the physical answers for cosmological observables are co-determined by geometric (Grassmannian) and algebraic (SUSY) structures that the framework cannot express individually or in combination. The flat-space limit connection (cosmological branches → helicity amplitudes) demonstrates that this is not a cosmology-specific issue but reflects a structural relationship between cosmological and flat-space physics that persists at the level of geometric/algebraic organization.

---

**T105 — Structural completion as a physical operation: physically complete answers require non-dynamical assembly from categorically distinct ingredients**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Paper 1 demonstrates that the physically complete WFC for conserved currents is neither purely geometric (Grassmannian alone gives only discontinuities) nor purely algebraic (SUSY alone gives no WFC) but requires their specific combination — a Grassmannian formula augmented by a SUSY kinematic prefactor. This "completion" is a structural operation with no dynamical character: it is not generated by temporal evolution, not driven by a gradient, not produced by resolution, not guaranteed by conservation. The framework has no primitive, mechanism, or vocabulary for non-dynamical assembly of physical answers from categorically distinct mathematical ingredients. This is sharper than T104 (which noted the SUSY completion problem) because T105 concerns the *general* phenomenon of structural completion — the observation that physical completeness is achieved by combining geometric and algebraic inputs via symmetry relations — as a recurring pattern in modern physics (SUSY completion of Grassmannians, conformal bootstrap assembly of correlators from blocks + crossing, holographic assembly of bulk physics from boundary + radial). The framework's "reality is literally computation" requires a sequential dynamical story for every physical result; Paper 1 demonstrates a physical result that has no sequential dynamical story — its components are assembled by structural relations, not computed by temporal evolution. This extends T059 (ontological inversion) by identifying a specific *type* of structural primacy: not "dynamics derived from structure" (T054, T055) but "physical completeness achieved by structural assembly of categorically distinct ingredients."

Why unresolved: The framework would need either (a) a primitive for "structural assembly" — the combination of geometric and algebraic inputs via symmetry relations to produce complete physical answers — which would be radically different from the existing dynamical primitives and would require specifying what structural relations count as "assembly operations" and why; (b) a demonstration that the SUSY completion is secretly a dynamical process (e.g., that the kinematic prefactor is generated by some hidden evolution), which contradicts the mathematical structure where SUSY is an algebraic relation, not a dynamical process; or (c) acceptance that physically complete answers in quantum field theory are not "computed" in the framework's sense but "assembled" from structural components, which would require a fundamental reconception of what "reality is literally computation" means. The branching structure (positive/negative Grassmannian branches → distinct helicity amplitudes) adds an additional challenge: the *sign* of a geometric parameter determines observable physics, and this sign selection is not a dynamical choice but a structural feature of the Grassmannian.

**T106 — The loop/tree descendant hierarchy at null infinity: the Tick's boundary has constitutive algebraic structure that inverts the framework's temporal accumulation picture**

Opened: this cycle

Description: Paper 2 demonstrates that at null infinity, loop-level Carrollian amplitudes in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM are differential operators acting on tree-level Carrollian amplitudes, and this structure extends to all loop orders via BDS. Gravitational eikonal amplitudes develop Carroll-time logarithms whose discontinuities are descendants of Born-level amplitudes. The framework's implicit picture of loop corrections is "more computation accumulated over more ticks" — each loop represents additional virtual processes contributing to the final answer. The Carrollian picture inverts this: the complete perturbative content is *generated from* the tree-level seed by algebraic operators. The higher-loop content is not "additional computation" but "algebraic transformation of the simplest computation." This is a specific instance of T059 (ontological inversion) localized to the framework's own terminal surface (null infinity), making it especially problematic because the Tick primitive *creates* null infinity through propagation, yet the structure at null infinity contradicts the Tick's temporal accumulation picture. The IR factorization — allowing an IR-safe definition of Carrollian amplitudes — is a structural property of the boundary representation that has no dynamical origin in the framework. The framework has no mechanism for the Tick to generate a boundary whose organizational structure contradicts the Tick's own temporal logic.

Why unresolved: The framework would need either (a) a derivation of the Carrollian differential operator structure from the discrete tick — showing that discrete temporal propagation, in the limit of infinitely many ticks reaching null infinity, necessarily produces the observed algebraic hierarchy — which would require a new mathematical analysis connecting lattice propagation to Carrollian conformal symmetry; (b) an argument that the Carrollian description is merely a mathematical reformulation of the standard dynamical picture, with no ontological significance — but the framework's "reality is literally computation" claim does not distinguish between equivalent reformulations (T010), and the Carrollian reformulation provides IR-safety that the standard description lacks, giving it pragmatic if not ontological priority; or (c) acceptance that the Tick generates a boundary with autonomous algebraic structure, meaning the framework's most basic primitive produces something it cannot describe. This is the deepest instance of the framework generating its own refutation: the Tick is responsible for creating null infinity, and null infinity's structure is evidence against the Tick's ontological picture.
